NOTE|info|playoff_format|2021-22 single-elimination bracket; 2023+ wild-card singles + best-of-3 first round (third legs as needed) + single elimination, recorded per RSSSF.

Several early-season games were postponed and replayed in July; recorded under their played dates. 2026 held appendix rows (2026-07-22/25/31) are NOT duplicated here (auditor dedupes).

Row counts 2021=459, 2022=476, 2023=493, 2024=493 (minus 11 held 2024-10-19 rows), 2025=510, 2026=267.
